+175|      </div>`;
+176|  }
+177|  const players = [game.players.A, game.players.B];
+178|  const rows = [70, 150];
+179|  const pegRows = players.map((player, index) => {
+180|    const y = rows[index];
+181|    const current = player.pegs[1];
+182|    const previous = player.pegs[0];
+183|    return `
+184|      <g>
+185|        <text x="20" y="${y + 6}" fill="#bfd0c3" font-size="15" 
font-weight="700">${escapeHtml(player.name)}</text>
+186|        <line x1="72" y1="${y}" x2="1128" y2="${y}" 
stroke="rgba(255,255,255,0.18)" stroke-width="8" stroke-linecap="round"></line>
+187|        ${Array.from({ length: 122 }, (_, score) => {
+188|          const x = scoreToX(score);
+189|          const emphasis = score % 10 === 0 ? 3.4 : 2;
+190|          return `<circle cx="${x.toFixed(1)}" cy="${y}" r="${emphasis}" 
fill="rgba(255,255,255,0.25)"></circle>`;
+191|        }).join('')}
+192|        ${Array.from({ length: 13 }, (_, i) => {
+193|          const score = i * 10;
+194|          const x = scoreToX(score);
+195|          return `<text x="${x.toFixed(1)}" y="${y - 14}" 
text-anchor="middle" font-size="11" fill="#bfd0c3">${score}</text>`;
+196|        }).join('')}
+197|        <circle cx="${scoreToX(previous).toFixed(1)}" cy="${y - 9}" r="7" 
fill="#f3c96a" stroke="#4c3811" stroke-width="2"></circle>
+198|        <circle cx="${scoreToX(current).toFixed(1)}" cy="${y + 9}" r="7" 
fill="#8fd4a8" stroke="#1f4730" stroke-width="2"></circle>
+199|        <text x="1146" y="${y + 6}" fill="#f3c96a" font-size="15" 
font-weight="700">${current}</text>
+200|      </g>`;
+201|  }).join('');
+202|
+203|  return `
+204|    <div class="board">
+205|      <div class="board-top">
+206|        <div class="board-stat"><span class="small">Hand</span><span 
class="value">${game.handNumber}</span></div>
+207|        <div class="board-stat"><span class="small">Dealer</span><span 
class="value">${escapeHtml(game.dealerName)}</span></div>
+208|        <div class="board-stat"><span class="small">Turn</span><span 
class="value">${escapeHtml(game.turnName)}</span></div>
+209|        <div class="board-stat"><span class="small">Count</span><span 
class="value">${game.count}</span></div>
+210|      </div>
+211|      <svg class="peg-svg" viewBox="0 0 ${width} ${height}" role="img" 
aria-label="Cribbage peg board">
+212|        <rect x="8" y="18" width="1184" height="184" rx="20" fill="#214f36"
stroke="rgba(255,255,255,0.12)"></rect>
+213|        ${pegRows}
+214|      </svg>
+215|    </div>`;
+216|}
+217|
+218|function renderGameActions(game) {
+219|  if (!game) {
+220|    return `<div class="info">Open a saved table or start a new game to 
begin.</div>`;
+221|  }
+222|  if (game.status === 'finished') {
+223|    const winner = game.winner === 'A' ? game.players.A.name : 
game.players.B.name;
+224|    return `
+225|      <div class="notice">
+226|        <strong>${escapeHtml(winner)}</strong> has won the game. The table 
is locked until you start another one.
+227|      </div>`;
+228|  }
+229|
+230|  const viewer = game.players[app.viewer];
+231|  const seat = app.viewer;
+232|  const ownHand = viewer.hand || [];
+233|  const opponent = game.players[seat === 'A' ? 'B' : 'A'];
+234|  const canDiscard = game.phase === 'discard';
+235|  const canPlay = game.phase === 'play' && game.turn === seat;
+236|  const selection = app.discardSelections[seat] || new Set();
+237|  const selectedCards = Array.from(selection);
+238|  const legal = new Set(game.legalCards || []);
+239|
+240|  let controls = '';
+241|  if (canDiscard) {
+242|    controls = `
+243|      <div class="row between">
+244|        <div class="small">Select exactly two cards from your own hand, 
then discard them.</div>
+245|        <button type="button" data-action-discard ${selectedCards.length 
=== 2 ? '' : 'disabled'}>Discard selected</button>
+246|      </div>`;
+247|  } else if (canPlay) {
+248|    if (game.legalCards.length) {
+249|      controls = `<div class="small">Tap a legal card to play it. Count 
must stay at 31 or below.</div>`;
+250|    } else if (game.canGo) {
+251|      controls = `
+252|        <div class="row between">
+253|          <div class="small">No legal card fits. Say go to pass the 
count.</div>
+254|          <button type="button" data-action-go>Go</button>
+255|        </div>`;
+256|    } else {
+257|      controls = `<div class="small">Waiting for the other seat.</div>`;
+258|    }
+259|  } else if (game.phase === 'show') {
+260|    controls = `<div class="row between"><div class="small">Show complete. 
Start the next hand when ready.</div><button type="button" 
data-action-next-hand>Next hand</button></div>`;
+261|  }
+262|
+263|  return `
+264|    <div class="grid-two">
+265|      <div class="player-cards">
+266|        ${renderSeatBox('A', game, seat, legal, selectedCards)}
+267|        ${renderSeatBox('B', game, seat, legal, selectedCards)}
+268|      </div>
+269|      <div class="stack">
+270|        <div class="player-box">
+271|          <div class="player-head"><strong>Crib</strong><span 
class="small">${game.crib.reveal ? 'revealed' : 'face down'}</span></div>
+272|          <div class="card-grid">
+273|            ${renderCribCards(game, seat)}
+274|          </div>
+275|        </div>
+276|        <div class="player-box">
+277|          <div class="player-head"><strong>Turn and play</strong><span 
class="small">Revision ${game.revision}</span></div>
+278|          <div class="small">Phase: ${escapeHtml(game.phase)} · Dealer: 
${escapeHtml(game.dealerName)} · Turn: ${escapeHtml(game.turnName)} · Cut: 
${game.cut ? escapeHtml(cardLabel(game.cut)) : 'hidden'}</div>
+279|          <div style="margin-top:0.75rem">${controls}</div>
+280|        </div>
+281|        <div class="player-box">
+282|          <div class="player-head"><strong>Played cards</strong><span 
class="small">Public pegging pile</span></div>
+283|          <div class="card-grid">
+284|            ${game.pile.length ? game.pile.map((code) => `<div 
class="card-wrap">${cardSvg(code, false)}<span 
class="card-caption">${escapeHtml(cardLabel(code))}</span></div>`).join('') : 
'<div class="small">No cards on the pile yet.</div>'}
+285|          </div>
+286|        </div>
+287|      </div>
+288|    </div>
+289|    ${game.showBreakdown && game.showBreakdown.length ? 
renderShowBreakdown(game) : ''}
+290|    ${renderActionLog(game)}
+291|  `;
+292|}
+293|
+294|function renderSeatBox(seat, game, viewerSeat, legal, selectedCards) {
+295|  const player = game.players[seat];
+296|  const reveal = player.reveal;
+297|  const isViewer = seat === viewerSeat;
+298|  const title = `${player.name} · Seat ${seat}`;
+299|  const scoreText = `${player.score} points`;
+300|  let cardsHtml = '';
+301|  if (reveal) {
+302|    const hand = player.hand || [];
+303|    if (game.phase === 'discard') {
+304|      cardsHtml = `
+305|        <div class="card-grid">
+306|          ${hand.length ? hand.map((code) => renderSeatCard(code, seat, 
game, isViewer, legal, selectedCards)).join('') : '<div class="small">No cards 
left.</div>'}
+307|        </div>`;
+308|    } else if (game.phase === 'play') {
+309|      cardsHtml = `
+310|        <div class="card-grid">
+311|          ${hand.length ? hand.map((code) => renderSeatCard(code, seat, 
game, isViewer, legal, selectedCards)).join('') : '<div class="small">No cards 
left.</div>'}
+312|        </div>`;
+313|      if (game.turn === seat && game.phase === 'play') {
+314|        cardsHtml += `<div class="small" 
style="margin-top:0.4rem">${legal.size ? 'Legal cards are clickable.' : 'No 
legal card fits right now.'}</div>`;
+315|      }
+316|    } else {
+317|      cardsHtml = `<div class="card-grid">${hand.length ? hand.map((code) 
=> `<div class="card-wrap">${cardSvg(code, false)}<span 
class="card-caption">${escapeHtml(cardLabel(code))}</span></div>`).join('') : 
'<div class="small">No cards.</div>'}</div>`;
+318|    }
+319|  } else {
+320|    cardsHtml = renderFaceDownCards(player.handCount, `${player.handCount} 
cards hidden`);
+321|  }
+322|
+323|  const discards = game.crib.reveal || isViewer ? player.discards || [] : 
[];
+324|  const discardHtml = discards.length
+325|    ? `<div class="small" style="margin-top:0.6rem">Discarded: 
${discards.map(escapeHtml).join(' ')}</div>`
+326|    : '';
+327|
+328|  const played = player.playedCards && player.playedCards.length
+329|    ? `<div class="small" style="margin-top:0.45rem">Played: 
${player.playedCards.map(escapeHtml).join(' ')}</div>`
+330|    : '';
+331|
+332|  return `
+333|    <div class="player-box">
+334|      <div class="player-head">
+335|        <div>
+336|          <strong>${escapeHtml(title)}</strong>
+337|          <div class="small">Played ${player.played} · Won ${player.won} · 
Pegs ${player.pegs[0]} → ${player.pegs[1]}</div>
+338|        </div>
+339|        <div>${scoreText}</div>
+340|      </div>
+341|      <div class="small">${reveal ? 'Hand visible' : 'Hand hidden from this
seat view'}</div>
+342|      <div style="margin-top:0.6rem">${cardsHtml}</div>
+343|      ${discardHtml}
+344|      ${played}
+345|    </div>`;
+346|}
+347|
+348|function renderSeatCard(code, seat, game, isViewer, legal, selectedCards) {
+349|  if (game.phase === 'discard' && isViewer && seat === app.viewer) {
+350|    const selected = selectedCards.includes(code);
+351|    return renderCardButton(code, { selectable: true, selected, action: 
'discard', label: cardLabel(code) });
+352|  }
+353|  if (game.phase === 'play' && isViewer && seat === app.viewer && game.turn
=== seat && legal.has(code)) {
+354|    return renderCardButton(code, { selectable: true, action: 'play', 
label: cardLabel(code) });
+355|  }
+356|  return `<div class="card-wrap">${cardSvg(code, false)}<span 
class="card-caption">${escapeHtml(cardLabel(code))}</span></div>`;
+357|}
+358|
+359|function renderCribCards(game, viewerSeat) {
+360|  if (game.crib.reveal) {
+361|    const cards = game.crib.cards || [];
+362|    return cards.length ? cards.map((code) => `<div 
class="card-wrap">${cardSvg(code, false)}<span 
class="card-caption">${escapeHtml(cardLabel(code))}</span></div>`).join('') : 
'<div class="small">No crib cards yet.</div>';
+363|  }
+364|  const own = game.crib.knownByViewer || [];
+365|  const oppCount = (game.players.A.discards || []).length + 
(game.players.B.discards || []).length - own.length;
+366|  const known = own.map((code) => `<div class="card-wrap">${cardSvg(code, 
false)}<span 
class="card-caption">${escapeHtml(cardLabel(code))}</span></div>`).join('');
+367|  const hidden = oppCount > 0 ? renderFaceDownCards(oppCount, `${oppCount} 
hidden from Seat ${viewerSeat}`) : '';
+368|  return `${known}${hidden}` || '<div class="small">Crib will be revealed 
at the show.</div>';
+369|}

+371|function renderShowBreakdown(game) {
+372|  const rows = game.showBreakdown.map((entry) => {
+373|    const label = entry.seat === 'crib' ? 'Crib' : `${entry.name} 
(${entry.seat === game.dealer ? 'dealer' : 'non-dealer'})`;
+374|    return `
+375|      <div class="log-item">
+376|        <div class="row 
between"><strong>${escapeHtml(label)}</strong><span>${entry.score.total} 
points</span></div>
+377|        <div 
class="small">${escapeHtml(formatBreakdown(entry.score))}</div>
+378|      </div>`;
+379|  }).join('');
+380|  return `
+381|    <div class="player-box" style="margin-top:0.9rem">
+382|      <div class="player-head"><strong>Show breakdown</strong><span 
class="small">Non-dealer, dealer, crib</span></div>
+383|      <div class="log">${rows}</div>
+384|    </div>`;

+387|function renderActionLog(game) {
+388|  const events = (game.events || []).slice().reverse().slice(0, 10);
+389|  return `
+390|    <div class="player-box" style="margin-top:0.9rem">
+391|      <div class="player-head"><strong>Commentary</strong><span 
class="small">Latest events</span></div>
+392|      <div class="log">
+393|        ${events.length ? events.map((event) => `<div class="log-item"><div
class="small">${escapeHtml(event.at || '')}</div><div>${escapeHtml(event.text ||
'')}</div></div>`).join('') : '<div class="small">No commentary yet.</div>'}
+394|      </div>
+395|    </div>`;

+580|function renderLadder() {
+581|  const host = document.getElementById('ladder');

+620|async function fetchJson(url, options = {}) {
+621|  const response = await fetch(url, {
+622|    headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json', ...(options.headers || 
{}) },
+623|    ...options,
+624|  });
+625|  const data = await response.json().catch(() => ({}));
+626|  if (!response.ok || data.ok === false) {
+627|    const error = new Error(data.error || `Request failed: 
${response.status}`);
+628|    error.data = data;
+629|    error.status = response.status;
+630|    throw error;
+631|  }
+632|  return data;
+633|}

+635|async function loadBootstrap() {
+636|  app.boot = await fetchJson('/api/bootstrap');
+637|  if (!app.gameId && app.boot.openGames && app.boot.openGames.length) {
+638|    app.gameId = app.boot.openGames[0].id;
+639|  }
+640|  if (!app.bench.example && app.boot.scoredHands && 
app.boot.scoredHands.length) {
+641|    applyBenchExample(0, false);
+642|  }
+643|  renderAll();
+644|  if (app.gameId) {
+645|    await loadGame(app.gameId, { keepMessage: false });
+646|  } else {
+647|    setStatus('Choose a saved game or start a new one.', 'info');
+648|  }

+651|async function loadGame(gameId, { keepMessage = false } = {}) {
+652|  if (!gameId) return;
+653|  app.gameId = gameId;
+654|  localStorage.setItem('club.gameId', gameId);
+655|  if (!keepMessage) setStatus(`Loading game ${gameId}…`, 'info');
+656|  const data = await 
fetchJson(`/api/games/${encodeURIComponent(gameId)}?viewer=${app.viewer}`);
+657|  app.game = data.game;
+658|  resetSelections();
+659|  renderAll();
+660|  setStatus(`Loaded ${gameId}. ${app.game.phase === 'finished' ? 'The game 
is finished.' : 'The table is ready.'}`, 'info');
+661|}

+663|function buildStartBody(form) {
+664|  const formData = new FormData(form);
+665|  const practice = formData.get('practice') || '';
+666|  const members = {
+667|    A: String(formData.get('memberA') || ''),
+668|    B: String(formData.get('memberB') || ''),
+669|  };
+670|  const scores = {
+671|    A: Number(formData.get('scoreA') || 0),
+672|    B: Number(formData.get('scoreB') || 0),
+673|  };
+674|  const body = {
+675|    actionId: crypto.randomUUID(),
+676|    mode: practice ? 'practice' : 'normal',
+677|    practiceKey: practice || null,
+678|    members,
+679|    scores,
+680|    dealer: String(formData.get('dealer') || 'A'),
+681|  };
+682|  return body;
+683|}
+684|
+685|async function startGame(form) {
+686|  const body = buildStartBody(form);
+687|  if (app.saving) return;
+688|  app.saving = true;
+689|  savePending({ url: '/api/games', body, label: 'create game', gameId: '' 
});
+690|  renderAll();
+691|  try {
+692|    const data = await fetchJson('/api/games', { method: 'POST', body: 
JSON.stringify(body) });
+693|    savePending(null);
+694|    app.gameId = data.game.id;
+695|    localStorage.setItem('club.gameId', app.gameId);
+696|    app.game = data.game;
+697|    resetSelections();
+698|    setStatus(`Started ${data.game.kind} game ${data.game.id}.`, 'notice');
+699|    renderAll();
+700|  } catch (error) {
+701|    setStatus(error.message, 'error');
+702|    renderAll();
+703|  } finally {
+704|    app.saving = false;
+705|    renderAll();
+706|  }
+707|}
+708|
+709|async function submitAction(type, extra = {}) {
+710|  if (!app.game || app.saving) return;
+711|  const seat = app.viewer;
+712|  const body = {
+713|    actionId: crypto.randomUUID(),
+714|    expectedRevision: app.game.revision,
+715|    seat,
+716|    type,
+717|    ...extra,
+718|  };
+719|  app.saving = true;
+720|  savePending({ url: `/api/games/${app.game.id}/actions`, body, label: 
type, gameId: app.game.id });
+721|  renderAll();
+722|  try {
+723|    const data = await fetchJson(`/api/games/${app.game.id}/actions`, { 
method: 'POST', body: JSON.stringify(body) });
+724|    savePending(null);
+725|    app.game = data.game;
+726|    resetSelections();
+727|    setStatus(data.note || `${type} accepted.`, 'notice');
+728|    renderAll();
+729|  } catch (error) {
+730|    if (error.data && error.data.currentRevision != null && 
error.data.game) {
+731|      app.game = error.data.game;
+732|      localStorage.setItem('club.gameId', app.game.id);
+733|      resetSelections();
+734|      setStatus(error.message, 'error');
+735|      renderAll();
+736|    } else {
+737|      setStatus(error.message, 'error');
+738|      renderAll();
+739|    }
+740|  } finally {
+741|    app.saving = false;
+742|    renderAll();
+743|  }

+746|function discardSelectionToggle(card) {
+747|  if (!app.game) return;
+748|  const seat = app.viewer;
+749|  const phase = app.game.phase;
+750|  if (phase !== 'discard') return;
+751|  const set = app.discardSelections[seat] || new Set();
+752|  if (set.has(card)) set.delete(card);
+753|  else {
+754|    if (set.size >= 2) return;
+755|    set.add(card);
+756|  }
+757|  app.discardSelections[seat] = set;
+758|  renderAll();
+759|}
+760|
+761|async function discardSelected() {
+762|  if (!app.game) return;
+763|  const seat = app.viewer;
+764|  const cards = Array.from(app.discardSelections[seat] || []);
+765|  if (cards.length !== 2) {
+766|    setStatus('Select exactly two cards to discard.', 'error');
+767|    return;
+768|  }
+769|  await submitAction('discard', { cards });
+770|}
+771|
+772|async function retryPending() {
+773|  if (!app.pending || app.saving) return;
+774|  app.saving = true;
+775|  renderAll();
+776|  try {
+777|    const data = await fetchJson(app.pending.url, { method: 'POST', body: 
JSON.stringify(app.pending.body) });
+778|    savePending(null);
+779|    if (data.game) {
+780|      app.game = data.game;
+781|      localStorage.setItem('club.gameId', data.game.id);
+782|    }
+783|    resetSelections();
+784|    setStatus(`${app.pending.label} confirmed.`, 'notice');
+785|    renderAll();
+786|  } catch (error) {
+787|    setStatus(error.message, 'error');
+788|    renderAll();
+789|  } finally {
+790|    app.saving = false;
+791|    renderAll();
+792|  }

+795|async function scoreBenchHand(form) {
+796|  const formData = new FormData(form);
+797|  const hand = [formData.get('hand1'), formData.get('hand2'), 
formData.get('hand3'), formData.get('hand4')].map((card) => String(card || 
'').trim().toUpperCase());
+798|  const cut = String(formData.get('cut') || '').trim().toUpperCase();
+799|  const crib = String(formData.get('crib') || 'false') === 'true';
+800|  const body = { hand, cut, crib };
+801|  try {
+802|    const data = await fetchJson('/api/score/hand', { method: 'POST', body:
JSON.stringify(body) });
+803|    app.bench.hand = hand;
+804|    app.bench.cut = cut;
+805|    app.bench.crib = crib;
+806|    app.benchResult = { ...data, mode: 'hand' };
+807|    setStatus(`Bench hand scored: ${data.total} points.`, 'notice');
+808|    renderAll();
+809|  } catch (error) {
+810|    setStatus(error.message, 'error');
+811|    renderAll();
+812|  }
+813|}
+814|
+815|async function scoreBenchPegging(form) {
+816|  const formData = new FormData(form);
+817|  const pile = String(formData.get('pile') || '')
+818|    .split(/[\s,]+/)
+819|    .map((card) => card.trim().toUpperCase())
+820|    .filter(Boolean);
+821|  const card = String(formData.get('card') || '').trim().toUpperCase();
+822|  const body = { pile, card };
+823|  try {
+824|    const data = await fetchJson('/api/score/pegging', { method: 'POST', 
body: JSON.stringify(body) });
+825|    app.bench.pile = pile.join(' ');
+826|    app.bench.card = card;
+827|    app.benchResult = { ...data, mode: 'peg' };
+828|    setStatus(`Pegging bench scored: ${data.points} points.`, 'notice');
+829|    renderAll();
+830|  } catch (error) {
+831|    setStatus(error.message, 'error');
+832|    renderAll();
+833|  }

+836|function applyBenchExample(index, scoreIt = true) {
+837|  if (!app.boot || !app.boot.scoredHands || !app.boot.scoredHands[index]) 
return;
+838|  const example = app.boot.scoredHands[index];
+839|  app.bench.example = String(index);
+840|  app.bench.hand = example.hand.slice();
+841|  app.bench.cut = example.cut;
+842|  app.bench.crib = !!example.crib;
+843|  app.benchResult = scoreIt ? { mode: 'hand', hand: example.hand.slice(), 
cut: example.cut, crib: !!example.crib, total: example.total, breakdown: null } 
: app.benchResult;
+844|  const handForm = document.getElementById('bench-hand-form');
+845|  if (handForm) {
+846|    handForm.elements.hand1.value = example.hand[0];
+847|    handForm.elements.hand2.value = example.hand[1];
+848|    handForm.elements.hand3.value = example.hand[2];
+849|    handForm.elements.hand4.value = example.hand[3];
+850|    handForm.elements.cut.value = example.cut;
+851|    handForm.elements.crib.value = String(!!example.crib);
+852|  }
+853|  if (scoreIt) {
+854|    scoreBenchHand(document.getElementById('bench-hand-form'));
+855|  } else {
+856|    renderAll();
+857|  }

+860|function bindEvents() {
+861|  document.getElementById('seat-a')?.addEventListener('click', () => 
setViewer('A'));
+862|  document.getElementById('seat-b')?.addEventListener('click', () => 
setViewer('B'));
+863|
+864|  document.addEventListener('click', (event) => {
+865|    const target = event.target.closest('[data-open-game], 
[data-discard-card], [data-play-card], [data-action-discard], [data-action-go], 
[data-action-next-hand], [data-pending-retry]');
+866|    if (!target) return;
+867|    if (target.hasAttribute('data-open-game')) {
+868|      loadGame(target.getAttribute('data-open-game'));
+869|      return;
+870|    }
+871|    if (target.hasAttribute('data-discard-card')) {
+872|      discardSelectionToggle(target.getAttribute('data-discard-card'));
+873|      return;
+874|    }
+875|    if (target.hasAttribute('data-play-card')) {
+876|      const card = target.getAttribute('data-play-card');
+877|      submitAction('play', { card });
+878|      return;
+879|    }
+880|    if (target.hasAttribute('data-action-discard')) {
+881|      discardSelected();
+882|      return;
+883|    }
+884|    if (target.hasAttribute('data-action-go')) {
+885|      submitAction('go');
+886|      return;
+887|    }
+888|    if (target.hasAttribute('data-action-next-hand')) {
+889|      submitAction('next-hand');
+890|      return;
+891|    }
+892|    if (target.hasAttribute('data-pending-retry')) {
+893|      retryPending();
+894|    }
+895|  });
+896|
+897|  document.addEventListener('submit', (event) => {
+898|    const form = event.target;
+899|    if (!(form instanceof HTMLFormElement)) return;
+900|    if (form.id === 'new-game-form-el') {
+901|      event.preventDefault();
+902|      startGame(form);
+903|      return;
+904|    }
+905|    if (form.id === 'bench-hand-form') {
+906|      event.preventDefault();
+907|      scoreBenchHand(form);
+908|      return;
+909|    }
+910|    if (form.id === 'bench-pegging-form') {
+911|      event.preventDefault();
+912|      scoreBenchPegging(form);
+913|    }
+914|  });
+915|
+916|  document.addEventListener('change', (event) => {
+917|    const target = event.target;
+918|    if (!(target instanceof HTMLSelectElement)) return;
+919|    if (target.id === 'bench-example') {
+920|      const index = Number(target.value);
+921|      if (!Number.isNaN(index)) applyBenchExample(index, true);
+922|      return;
+923|    }
+924|  });
+925|}
+926|
+927|async function init() {
+928|  bindEvents();
+929|  renderAll();
+930|  try {
+931|    await loadBootstrap();
+932|    if (app.pending) {
+933|      setStatus('A save is pending. You can retry it now.', 'info');
+934|    }
+935|    if (app.gameId && !app.game) {
+936|      await loadGame(app.gameId);
+937|    }
+938|  } catch (error) {
+939|    setStatus(error.message, 'error');
+940|    renderAll();
+941|  }
+942|}

   114  function renderCardButton(code, { selectable = false, selected = false, 
action = 'discard', disabled = false, label = '' } = {}) {
   115    const title = label || cardLabel(code);
   116    const actionAttr = action === 'play' ? `data-play-card="${code}"` : 
`data-discard-card="${code}"`;
   117    const cls = ['card-btn'];
   118    if (selected) cls.push('selected');
   119    if (disabled) cls.push('disabled');
   120    return `
   121      <button type="button" class="${cls.join(' ')}" 
aria-label="${escapeHtml(title)}" ${selectable ? actionAttr : ''} ${disabled ? 
'disabled' : ''}>
   122        <div class="card-wrap">
   123          ${cardSvg(code, false)}
   124          <span class="card-caption">${escapeHtml(title)}</span>
   125        </div>
   126      </button>`;
   127  }

   129  function renderFaceDownCards(count, caption = '') {
   130    const cards = [];
   131    for (let i = 0; i < count; i += 1) cards.push(cardSvg(`back-${i}`, 
true));
   132    return `
   133      <div class="hidden-hand">
   134        <div class="card-grid" style="grid-template-columns: 
repeat(auto-fit, minmax(64px, 64px)); gap: 0.35rem;">${cards.map((svg) => 
`<div>${svg}</div>`).join('')}</div>
   135        <span class="small">${escapeHtml(caption || `${count} cards face 
down`)}</span>
   136      </div>`;
   137  }

   139  function formatBreakdown(score) {
   140    const b = score.breakdown;
   141    const parts = [];
   142    if (b.fifteens) parts.push(`fifteens ${b.fifteens}`);
   143    if (b.pairs) parts.push(`pairs ${b.pairs}`);
   144    if (b.runs) parts.push(`runs ${b.runs}`);
   145    if (b.flush) parts.push(`flush ${b.flush}`);
   146    if (b.nobs) parts.push(`nobs ${b.nobs}`);
   147    return parts.length ? parts.join(', ') : 'nothing scores';
   148  }

   620  async function fetchJson(url, options = {}) {
   621    const response = await fetch(url, {
   622      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json', ...(options.headers 
|| {}) },
   623      ...options,
   624    });
   625    const data = await response.json().catch(() => ({}));
   626    if (!response.ok || data.ok === false) {
   627      const error = new Error(data.error || `Request failed: 
${response.status}`);
   628      error.data = data;
   629      error.status = response.status;
   630      throw error;

   772  async function retryPending() {
   773    if (!app.pending || app.saving) return;
   774    app.saving = true;
   775    renderAll();
   776    try {
   777      const data = await fetchJson(app.pending.url, { method: 'POST', 
body: JSON.stringify(app.pending.body) });
   778      savePending(null);
   779      if (data.game) {
   780        app.game = data.game;
   781        localStorage.setItem('club.gameId', data.game.id);
   782      }
   783      resetSelections();
   784      setStatus(`${app.pending.label} confirmed.`, 'notice');
   785      renderAll();
   786    } catch (error) {
   787      setStatus(error.message, 'error');
   788      renderAll();
   789    } finally {
   790      app.saving = false;
   791      renderAll();
   792    }
   793  }
   794  
   795  async function scoreBenchHand(form) {
   796    const formData = new FormData(form);
   797    const hand = [formData.get('hand1'), formData.get('hand2'), 
formData.get('hand3'), formData.get('hand4')].map((card) => String(card || 
'').trim().toUpperCase());
   798    const cut = String(formData.get('cut') || '').trim().toUpperCase();
   799    const crib = String(formData.get('crib') || 'false') === 'true';
   800    const body = { hand, cut, crib };
   801    try {
   802      const data = await fetchJson('/api/score/hand', { method: 'POST', 
body: JSON.stringify(body) });
   803      app.bench.hand = hand;
   804      app.bench.cut = cut;
   805      app.bench.crib = crib;
   806      app.benchResult = { ...data, mode: 'hand' };
   807      setStatus(`Bench hand scored: ${data.total} points.`, 'notice');
   808      renderAll();
   809    } catch (error) {
   810      setStatus(error.message, 'error');
   811      renderAll();
   812    }
   813  }
   814  
   815  async function scoreBenchPegging(form) {
   816    const formData = new FormData(form);
   817    const pile = String(formData.get('pile') || '')
   818      .split(/[\s,]+/)
   819      .map((card) => card.trim().toUpperCase())
   820      .filter(Boolean);

Action: FileEditorAction

Arguments:
  command: "create"
  path: "/app/APP_MANIFEST.md"
  file_text: 
    # Gambit Hollow app manifest
    
    ```bash
    node serve.js
    ```
    
    - SQLite database: `/app/gambit.db`
    - The server also honors `DB_PATH` for a relocated runtime database.
    - Health probe: `GET /api/health`
    
    ## Main read routes
    
    - `GET /api/bootstrap` — members, open games, history, practice data, 
scoring examples
    - `GET /api/games` — all games plus the open-game chooser list
    - `GET /api/games/:id?viewer=A|B` — read one game from a seat view and get 
its nonnegative revision
    - `GET /api/history` — historical summaries
    - `GET /api/history/:id` — one historical summary record
    
    ## Scoring routes
    
    - `POST /api/score/hand`
      - body: `{ "hand": ["5S","5H","5D","JC"], "cut": "5C", "crib": false }`
      - returns total plus fifteens, pairs, runs, flush and nobs
    - `POST /api/score/pegging`
      - body: `{ "pile": ["7H","TH"], "card": "2S" }`
      - returns count before/after, points, reasons and pegging breakdown
    
    ## Game action routes
    
    - `POST /api/games`
      - body includes `actionId`, `mode`, `practiceKey`, `members`, `scores`, 
and `dealer`
      - creating a game is idempotent on `actionId`
    - `POST /api/games/:id/actions`
      - body includes `actionId`, `expectedRevision`, `seat`, `type`, and 
action-specific fields
      - supported types: `discard`, `play`, `go`, `next-hand`
      - the browser sends a client-generated `actionId` and the revision it last
read
      - repeated identical requests return the original acceptance; stale 
revisions are refused
    
    ## Reading revisions
    
    Every game read exposes `revision` in the JSON payload from `GET 
/api/games/:id` and the chooser routes. The client reads that value and sends it
back as `expectedRevision` for all mutating game actions.
    

  kind: "FileEditorAction"

Summary: Compare scoring endpoint against examples

Thought:
I’m validating the scoring endpoint against all reference hands.

$ node - <<'NODE'
const { SCORED_HANDS } = require('/assets/club/scored-hands.js');
(async () => {
  let failures = 0;
  for (const [i, ex] of SCORED_HANDS.entries()) {
    const res = await fetch('http://127.0.0.1:3000/api/score/hand', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify({ hand: ex.hand, cut: ex.cut, crib: ex.crib })
    });
    const data = await res.json();
    if (!data.ok || data.total !== ex.total) {
      console.log('FAIL', i + 1, ex.hand.join(' '), ex.cut, 'expected', 
ex.total, 'got', data.total, data.error || '');
      failures += 1;
    }
  }
  console.log('failures', failures);
  process.exit(failures ? 1 : 0);
})();
NODE [timeout: 30.0s]

Summary: Run Playwright cribbage browser flow

$ node - <<'NODE'
const { createRequire } = require('node:module');
const playwright = 
createRequire(require.resolve('@playwright/mcp'))('playwright');

(async () => {
  const browser = await playwright.chromium.launch({ executablePath: 
'/usr/local/bin/chromium', headless: true, args: ['--no-sandbox'] });
  const context = await browser.newContext();
  const errors = [];
  const attach = (page, name) => {
    page.on('console', (msg) => {
      const text = msg.text();
      if (['error', 'warning'].includes(msg.type())) {
        errors.push(`${name}:${msg.type()}:${text}`);
      }
      console.log(`${name}:console:${msg.type()}:${text}`);
    });
    page.on('pageerror', (err) => {
      errors.push(`${name}:pageerror:${err.message}`);
      console.log(`${name}:pageerror:${err.message}`);
    });
  };

  const page1 = await context.newPage();
  attach(page1, 'p1');
  await page1.goto('http://127.0.0.1:3000/');
  await page1.waitForSelector('text=Start game', { timeout: 15000 });
  await page1.getByRole('button', { name: 'Start game' }).click();
  await page1.waitForSelector('#game-view', { timeout: 15000 });
  await page1.waitForSelector('[data-discard-card]', { timeout: 15000 });
  const aDiscards = page1.locator('[data-discard-card]');
  const aCount = await aDiscards.count();
  if (aCount < 2) throw new Error(`expected at least 2 discard cards for seat A,
got ${aCount}`);
  await aDiscards.nth(0).click();
  await aDiscards.nth(1).click();
  await page1.getByRole('button', { name: 'Discard selected' }).click();
  await page1.waitForFunction(() => document.body.textContent.includes('Phase: 
play'));
  await page1.getByRole('button', { name: 'Seat B view' }).click();
  await page1.waitForFunction(() => document.body.textContent.includes('Seat B 
view'));
  await page1.waitForSelector('[data-discard-card]', { timeout: 15000 });
  const bDiscards = page1.locator('[data-discard-card]');
  const bCount = await bDiscards.count();
  if (bCount < 2) throw new Error(`expected at least 2 discard cards for seat B,
got ${bCount}`);
  await bDiscards.nth(0).click();
  await bDiscards.nth(1).click();
  await page1.getByRole('button', { name: 'Discard selected' }).click();
  await page1.waitForFunction(() => document.body.textContent.includes('Phase: 
play'));
  await page1.waitForSelector('[data-play-card]', { timeout: 15000 });
  const playB = page1.locator('[data-play-card]');
  const playBCount = await playB.count();
  if (playBCount < 1) throw new Error('expected a legal play for seat B');
  await playB.nth(0).click();
  await page1.waitForFunction(() => !document.body.textContent.includes('No 
cards on the pile yet.') || document.body.textContent.includes('No legal card 
fits right now.') || document.body.textContent.includes('Waiting for the other 
seat.'));

  await page1.reload({ waitUntil: 'networkidle' });
  await page1.waitForSelector('#game-view', { timeout: 15000 });
  await page1.waitForFunction(() => document.body.textContent.includes('Game 
state and pegs'));

  const page2 = await context.newPage();
  attach(page2, 'p2');
  await page2.goto('http://127.0.0.1:3000/');
  await page2.waitForSelector('#game-view', { timeout: 15000 });
  await page2.waitForSelector('text=Revision', { timeout: 15000 });

  // Use whichever seat currently has the turn.
  const turnText = await page2.locator('.board-stat 
.value').nth(2).textContent();
  const seatButton = turnText && turnText.includes('Seat B') ? 'Seat B view' : 
'Seat A view';
  await page2.getByRole('button', { name: seatButton }).click();
  await page2.waitForTimeout(250);
  const playable = page2.locator('[data-play-card]');
  const playableCount = await playable.count();
  if (playableCount < 1) {
    const otherButton = seatButton === 'Seat B view' ? 'Seat A view' : 'Seat B 
view';
    await page2.getByRole('button', { name: otherButton }).click();
    await page2.waitForTimeout(250);
  }
  const finalPlayables = page2.locator('[data-play-card]');
  const finalCount = await finalPlayables.count();
  if (finalCount < 1) throw new Error('expected a playable card after reload');
  await finalPlayables.nth(0).click();
  await page2.waitForTimeout(500);

  console.log('BROWSER FLOW OK');
  if (errors.length) {
    console.log('Captured errors:', JSON.stringify(errors, null, 2));
    process.exit(1);
  }
  await browser.close();
})();

reloads.</div>
   496        </div>`;
   497      return;
   498    }
   499    host.innerHTML = renderGameActions(app.game);
   500  }
   501  
   502  function renderBench() {
   503    const host = document.getElementById('bench');
   504    if (!host) return;
   505    const examples = (app.boot && app.boot.scoredHands) || [];
   506    const exampleOptions = ['<option value="">Choose a reference 
example</option>'].concat(examples.map((example, index) => {
   507      const name = `${example.total} points · ${example.hand.join(' ')} / 
${example.cut}`;
   508      const selected = String(index) === app.bench.example ? ' selected' :
'';
   509      return `<option value="${index}"${selected}>${escapeHtml(name)} — 
${escapeHtml(example.rule)}</option>`;
   510    })).join('');

const { createRequire } = require('node:module');
const playwright = 
createRequire(require.resolve('@playwright/mcp'))('playwright');

(async () => {
  const browser = await playwright.chromium.launch({ executablePath: 
'/usr/local/bin/chromium', headless: true, args: ['--no-sandbox'] });
  const context = await browser.newContext();
  const errors = [];
  const attach = (page, name) => {
    page.on('console', (msg) => {
      const text = msg.text();
      if (['error', 'warning'].includes(msg.type())) 
errors.push(`${name}:${msg.type()}:${text}`);
      console.log(`${name}:console:${msg.type()}:${text}`);
    });
    page.on('pageerror', (err) => {
      errors.push(`${name}:pageerror:${err.message}`);
      console.log(`${name}:pageerror:${err.message}`);
    });
  };

  const page1 = await context.newPage();
  attach(page1, 'p1');
  await page1.goto('http://127.0.0.1:3000/');
  await page1.waitForSelector('text=Start game', { timeout: 15000 });
  await page1.getByRole('button', { name: 'Start game' }).click();

got ${aCount}`);

got ${bCount}`);
  await bDiscards.nth(0).click();

play'));

Summary: Run corrected Playwright browser flow

$ node - <<'NODE'
const { createRequire } = require('node:module');
const playwright = 
createRequire(require.resolve('@playwright/mcp'))('playwright');

(async () => {
  const browser = await playwright.chromium.launch({ executablePath: 
'/usr/local/bin/chromium', headless: true, args: ['--no-sandbox'] });
  const context = await browser.newContext();
  const errors = [];
  const attach = (page, name) => {
    page.on('console', (msg) => {
      const text = msg.text();
      if (['error', 'warning'].includes(msg.type())) 
errors.push(`${name}:${msg.type()}:${text}`);
      console.log(`${name}:console:${msg.type()}:${text}`);
    });
    page.on('pageerror', (err) => {
      errors.push(`${name}:pageerror:${err.message}`);
      console.log(`${name}:pageerror:${err.message}`);
    });
  };

  const page1 = await context.newPage();
  attach(page1, 'p1');
  await page1.goto('http://127.0.0.1:3000/');
  await page1.waitForSelector('text=Start game', { timeout: 15000 });
  await page1.getByRole('button', { name: 'Start game' }).click();
  await page1.waitForSelector('[data-discard-card]', { timeout: 15000 });
  const aDiscards = page1.locator('[data-discard-card]');
  const aCount = await aDiscards.count();
  console.log('seat A discard buttons', aCount);
  if (aCount < 2) throw new Error(`expected at least 2 discard cards for seat A,
got ${aCount}`);
  await aDiscards.nth(0).click();
  await aDiscards.nth(1).click();
  await page1.getByRole('button', { name: 'Discard selected' }).click();
  await page1.waitForSelector('text=Phase: discard');
  await page1.getByRole('button', { name: 'Seat B view' }).click();
  await page1.waitForSelector('[data-discard-card]', { timeout: 15000 });
  const bDiscards = page1.locator('[data-discard-card]');
  const bCount = await bDiscards.count();
  console.log('seat B discard buttons', bCount);
  if (bCount < 2) throw new Error(`expected at least 2 discard cards for seat B,
got ${bCount}`);
  await bDiscards.nth(0).click();
  await bDiscards.nth(1).click();
  await page1.getByRole('button', { name: 'Discard selected' }).click();
  await page1.waitForFunction(() => document.body.textContent.includes('Phase: 
play'));
  await page1.waitForSelector('[data-play-card]', { timeout: 15000 });
  const playB = page1.locator('[data-play-card]');
  const playBCount = await playB.count();
  console.log('seat B play buttons', playBCount);
  if (playBCount < 1) throw new Error('expected a legal play for seat B');
  await playB.nth(0).click();

  await page1.reload({ waitUntil: 'networkidle' });
  await page1.waitForSelector('#game-view', { timeout: 15000 });
  await page1.waitForFunction(() => document.body.textContent.includes('Game 
state and pegs'));

  const page2 = await context.newPage();
  attach(page2, 'p2');
  await page2.goto('http://127.0.0.1:3000/');
  await page2.waitForSelector('#game-view', { timeout: 15000 });
  await page2.waitForFunction(() => document.body.textContent.includes('Game 
state and pegs'));
  const turnText = await page2.locator('.board-stat 
.value').nth(2).textContent();
  const seatButton = turnText && turnText.includes('Seat B') ? 'Seat B view' : 
'Seat A view';
  await page2.getByRole('button', { name: seatButton }).click();
  await page2.waitForTimeout(250);
  let finalPlayables = page2.locator('[data-play-card]');
  let finalCount = await finalPlayables.count();
  if (finalCount < 1) {
    const otherButton = seatButton === 'Seat B view' ? 'Seat A view' : 'Seat B 
view';
    await page2.getByRole('button', { name: otherButton }).click();
    await page2.waitForTimeout(250);
    finalPlayables = page2.locator('[data-play-card]');
    finalCount = await finalPlayables.count();
  }
  console.log('fresh page play buttons', finalCount);
  if (finalCount < 1) throw new Error('expected a playable card after reload');
  await finalPlayables.nth(0).click();
  await page2.waitForTimeout(500);

  console.log('BROWSER FLOW OK');
  if (errors.length) {
    console.log('Captured errors:', JSON.stringify(errors, null, 2));
    process.exit(1);
  }
  await browser.close();
})();

(async () => {
  const before = await (await 
fetch('http://127.0.0.1:3000/api/bootstrap')).json();
  const start = await (await fetch('http://127.0.0.1:3000/api/games', {
    method: 'POST',
    headers: {'Content-Type':'application/json'},
    body: JSON.stringify({ actionId: crypto.randomUUID(), mode:'practice', 
practiceKey:'heels', members:{A:'M-003', B:'M-014'}, scores:{A:120,B:0}, 
dealer:'A' })
  })).json();
  const gid = start.game.id;
  let game = (await (await 
fetch(`http://127.0.0.1:3000/api/games/${gid}?viewer=A`)).json()).game;
  let res = await (await fetch(`http://127.0.0.1:3000/api/games/${gid}/actions`,
{ method:'POST', headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'}, body: 
JSON.stringify({ actionId: crypto.randomUUID(), expectedRevision: game.revision,
seat:'A', type:'discard', cards: game.players.A.hand.slice(0,2) }) })).json();
  console.log('A discard', res.ok, res.game.phase, res.game.revision);
  game = (await (await 
fetch(`http://127.0.0.1:3000/api/games/${gid}?viewer=B`)).json()).game;
  res = await (await fetch(`http://127.0.0.1:3000/api/games/${gid}/actions`, { 
method:'POST', headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'}, body: 
JSON.stringify({ actionId: crypto.randomUUID(), expectedRevision: game.revision,
seat:'B', type:'discard', cards: game.players.B.hand.slice(0,2) }) })).json();
  console.log('B discard', res.ok, res.game.phase, res.game.status, 
res.game.winner, res.game.players.A.score, res.game.players.B.score);
  const after = await (await 
fetch('http://127.0.0.1:3000/api/bootstrap')).json();
  const mA2 = after.members.find((m) => m.no === 'M-003');
  const mB2 = after.members.find((m) => m.no === 'M-014');
  console.log('after ladder', mA2.played, mA2.won, mB2.played, mB2.won);
})();

$ node - <<'NODE'
async function createPractice(key) {
  const res = await fetch('http://127.0.0.1:3000/api/games', {
    method: 'POST',
    headers: {'Content-Type': 'application/json'},
    body: JSON.stringify({ actionId: crypto.randomUUID(), mode: 'practice', 
practiceKey: key, members: { A: 'M-003', B: 'M-014' }, scores: { A: 0, B: 0 }, 
dealer: 'A' })
  });
  return (await res.json()).game;
}
async function discardTwo(gameId, seat) {
  const game = (await (await 
fetch(`http://127.0.0.1:3000/api/games/${gameId}?viewer=${seat}`)).json()).game;
  const cards = game.players[seat].hand.slice(4, 6);
  const res = await fetch(`http://127.0.0.1:3000/api/games/${gameId}/actions`, {
    method: 'POST',
    headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
    body: JSON.stringify({ actionId: crypto.randomUUID(), expectedRevision: 
game.revision, seat, type: 'discard', cards })
  });
  return res.json();
}
async function playCard(gameId, seat, card) {
  const game = (await (await 
fetch(`http://127.0.0.1:3000/api/games/${gameId}?viewer=${seat}`)).json()).game;
  if (game.turn === seat && !(game.legalCards || []).includes(card)) {
    const go = await fetch(`http://127.0.0.1:3000/api/games/${gameId}/actions`, 
{
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify({ actionId: crypto.randomUUID(), expectedRevision: 
game.revision, seat, type: 'go' })
    });
    const goData = await go.json();
    if (!goData.ok) throw new Error(`go failed for ${seat}: ${goData.error}`);
  }
  const fresh = (await (await 
fetch(`http://127.0.0.1:3000/api/games/${gameId}?viewer=${seat}`)).json()).game;
  const res = await fetch(`http://127.0.0.1:3000/api/games/${gameId}/actions`, {
    method: 'POST',
    headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
    body: JSON.stringify({ actionId: crypto.randomUUID(), expectedRevision: 
fresh.revision, seat, type: 'play', card })
  });
  const data = await res.json();
  if (!data.ok) throw new Error(`${seat} play ${card} failed: ${data.error}`);
  return data.game;
}
(async () => {
  for (const key of ['tail', 'last31']) {
    const game = await createPractice(key);
    await discardTwo(game.id, 'A');
    await discardTwo(game.id, 'B');
    const sequence = key === 'tail'
      ? 
[['B','AS'],['A','TS'],['B','2H'],['A','JH'],['B','3D'],['B','4C'],['A','QD'],['
A','KC']]
      : 
[['B','5S'],['A','6H'],['B','TD'],['A','TC'],['B','8S'],['A','9H'],['B','7D'],['
A','7C']];
    let state = null;
    for (const [seat, card] of sequence) {
      state = await playCard(game.id, seat, card);
    }
    console.log(key, 'phase', state.phase, 'status', state.status, 'winner', 
state.winner, 'scores', state.players.A.score, state.players.B.score);
  }
})();

Summary: Rerun browser flow after go fix

Thought:
I’m rerunning the browser flow once more after the go fix.

$ node - <<'NODE'
const { createRequire } = require('node:module');
const playwright = 
createRequire(require.resolve('@playwright/mcp'))('playwright');

(async () => {
  const browser = await playwright.chromium.launch({ executablePath: 
'/usr/local/bin/chromium', headless: true, args: ['--no-sandbox'] });
  const context = await browser.newContext();
  const errors = [];
  const attach = (page, name) => {
    page.on('console', (msg) => {
      const text = msg.text();
      if (['error', 'warning'].includes(msg.type())) 
errors.push(`${name}:${msg.type()}:${text}`);
    });
    page.on('pageerror', (err) => {
      errors.push(`${name}:pageerror:${err.message}`);
    });
  };

  const page1 = await context.newPage();
  attach(page1, 'p1');
  await page1.goto('http://127.0.0.1:3000/');
  await page1.waitForSelector('text=Start game', { timeout: 15000 });
  await page1.getByRole('button', { name: 'Start game' }).click();
  await page1.waitForSelector('[data-discard-card]', { timeout: 15000 });
  const aDiscards = page1.locator('[data-discard-card]');
  await aDiscards.nth(0).click();
  await aDiscards.nth(1).click();
  await page1.getByRole('button', { name: 'Discard selected' }).click();
  await page1.waitForSelector('text=Phase: discard');
  await page1.getByRole('button', { name: 'Seat B view' }).click();
  await page1.waitForSelector('[data-discard-card]', { timeout: 15000 });
  const bDiscards = page1.locator('[data-discard-card]');
  await bDiscards.nth(0).click();
  await bDiscards.nth(1).click();
  await page1.getByRole('button', { name: 'Discard selected' }).click();
  await page1.waitForFunction(() => document.body.textContent.includes('Phase: 
play'));
  await page1.waitForSelector('[data-play-card]', { timeout: 15000 });
  await page1.locator('[data-play-card]').first().click();

  await page1.reload({ waitUntil: 'networkidle' });
  await page1.waitForSelector('#game-view', { timeout: 15000 });

  const page2 = await context.newPage();
  attach(page2, 'p2');
  await page2.goto('http://127.0.0.1:3000/');
  await page2.waitForSelector('#game-view', { timeout: 15000 });
  const turnText = await page2.locator('.board-stat 
.value').nth(2).textContent();
  const seatButton = turnText && turnText.includes('Seat B') ? 'Seat B view' : 
'Seat A view';
  await page2.getByRole('button', { name: seatButton }).click();
  await page2.waitForTimeout(250);
  let finalPlayables = page2.locator('[data-play-card]');
  let finalCount = await finalPlayables.count();
  if (finalCount < 1) {
    const otherButton = seatButton === 'Seat B view' ? 'Seat A view' : 'Seat B 
view';
    await page2.getByRole('button', { name: otherButton }).click();
    await page2.waitForTimeout(250);
    finalPlayables = page2.locator('[data-play-card]');
    finalCount = await finalPlayables.count();
  }
  if (finalCount < 1) throw new Error('expected a playable card after reload');
  await finalPlayables.nth(0).click();
  await page2.waitForTimeout(500);

  if (errors.length) {
    console.log(JSON.stringify(errors, null, 2));
    process.exit(1);
  }
  console.log('BROWSER FLOW OK');
  await browser.close();
})();

$ node - <<'NODE'
const fs = require('fs');
const Database = require('better-sqlite3');
const seed = 
JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync('/assets/club/records/gambit_seed_data.json', 
'utf8'));
const db = new Database('/app/gambit.db');
db.transaction(() => {
  db.prepare('DELETE FROM games').run();
  db.prepare("DELETE FROM receipts").run();
  db.prepare("DELETE FROM history WHERE type = 'game'").run();
  const updateMember = db.prepare('UPDATE members SET played = ?, won = ? WHERE 
no = ?');
  for (const member of seed.members) {
    updateMember.run(member.played, member.won, member.no);
  }
})();
db.close();
